  10-decisions-log.md:341  [Named / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-06-20 — Adaptive runtime fusion: the recipe principle, two-tier extensibility, and the Fuel-strategist / backend-synthesizer JIT loop] mechanism is the declarative form
  10-decisions-log.md:343  [Directive / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-06-20 — Adaptive runtime fusion: the recipe principle, two-tier extensibility, and the Fuel-strategist / backend-synthesizer JIT loop] **Missing-fusion telemetry (G5).** Today there is **no** missing-fusion signal: Fuel can see fusions it *performed*, not fusions it *wanted-but-lacked* ("no rule fired" is identical across every primitive node, `opt.rs:256-273`). It needs a **new graph-layer hook** and depends on the base emission seam (`structure_key` is a 13-line stub; no `DispatchRecord` is emitted yet). Sequencing (canonical in [08-pattern-harvest](08-pattern-harvest.md) + `baracuda-telemetry-plan.md` §9): **closed-world `FusionMissRecord`** (a recognized fusion-eligible chain realized as N primitives because the kernel was absent — reason `NoBackendKernel`, against **known** `FusedOpId`s) is the v1 **headline**, built FIRST (its consumer — append a `BindingEntry` — already exists, Tier 1). **Open-world `SequenceRecord{fused_as: None}`** (a frequent realized op chain matching **no** known identity — discovered by *observation*, not subgraph *enumeration*) is **deferred**, because its consumer is the Tier-2 runtime declarative registration. We never enumerate the subgraph space and never search for a whole-model fusion.
  10-decisions-log.md:343  [Named / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-06-20 — Adaptive runtime fusion: the recipe principle, two-tier extensibility, and the Fuel-strategist / backend-synthesizer JIT loop] Open-world `SequenceRecord{fused_as: None}`
  10-decisions-log.md:345  [Directive / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-06-20 — Adaptive runtime fusion: the recipe principle, two-tier extensibility, and the Fuel-strategist / backend-synthesizer JIT loop] **Whole-model / megakernel is a real but NARROW technique (G6).** Supported when profiling justifies, but the **last / highest-risk target, never the default**. It wins *something* over even an ideal CUDA Graph (inter-kernel GPU scheduling bubbles + cross-boundary software pipelining) and removes host-round-trip risk, and *can* host multiple strategies via a persistent-megakernel. But the benefit curve **turns over** because of **fixed launch geometry** (one grid/block per launch), **kernel-global register allocation** (the worst region's register footprint is imposed on every region → bandwidth-bound regions are forced to the compute region's low occupancy; internal sub-kernels do **not** fix this — the register file is partitioned at launch by the kernel's static max), and **per-shape JIT/specialization combinatorics**. "Bigger fusion = better" is **not monotonic**. Captured-run replay (CUDA Graphs / pre-recorded command buffers, per [11-persistence](11-persistence.md)) is the cheaper step below it that already captures most of the launch-overhead win without fusing compute.
  10-decisions-log.md:347  [Named / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-06-20 — Adaptive runtime fusion: the recipe principle, two-tier extensibility, and the Fuel-strategist / backend-synthesizer JIT loop] Fuel is the STRATEGIST
  10-decisions-log.md:347  [Named / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-06-20 — Adaptive runtime fusion: the recipe principle, two-tier extensibility, and the Fuel-strategist / backend-synthesizer JIT loop] A backend (Baracuda) is the SYNTHESIZER
  10-decisions-log.md:347  [Named / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-06-20 — Adaptive runtime fusion: the recipe principle, two-tier extensibility, and the Fuel-strategist / backend-synthesizer JIT loop] win-rate flattening is the STOP signal
  10-decisions-log.md:349  [Directive / StatusNote] [Decisions log > 2026-06-20 — Adaptive runtime fusion: the recipe principle, two-tier extensibility, and the Fuel-strategist / backend-synthesizer JIT loop] **Kernel-cache pruning (G8).** Prune **rarely** — likely not at all initially. When forced, evict kernels that **lose across *every* model** first ("loses no matter which model considers it" = a never-useful proof). Gate eviction behind a **developer-set max-kernel-drive-space cap** — only evict under space pressure, so a kernel is never discarded while there is room (a currently-*shadowed* kernel that might win under a different cover, or in a different model, stays). Do **not** prune on a single model's losses — "winning" is relative to the current kernel set (shadowing). Canonical home: [11-persistence](11-persistence.md).
  10-decisions-log.md:354  [Directive / StatusNote] [Decisions log > 2026-06-20 — Adaptive runtime fusion: the recipe principle, two-tier extensibility, and the Fuel-strategist / backend-synthesizer JIT loop] *Let the backend find fusion opportunities and JIT them autonomously.* Rejected — that migrates strategy into the backend, violating the constitution; Baracuda synthesizes a Fuel-chosen region instead.
  10-decisions-log.md:355  [Directive / StatusNote] [Decisions log > 2026-06-20 — Adaptive runtime fusion: the recipe principle, two-tier extensibility, and the Fuel-strategist / backend-synthesizer JIT loop] *Send Baracuda the whole base map.* Rejected — wasteful and a strategy leak; Fuel sends partial, optimizable sub-regions, on Fuel's resource-aware schedule.
  10-decisions-log.md:356  [Directive / StatusNote] [Decisions log > 2026-06-20 — Adaptive runtime fusion: the recipe principle, two-tier extensibility, and the Fuel-strategist / backend-synthesizer JIT loop] *Greedy "largest region that fits first."* Rejected — that optimizes launch-count (the least-valuable axis) and is the lowest-frequency, highest-compile-cost, highest-fit-risk target; order by frequency-weighted profitability (small repeated motifs first), with empirical winning as the ground-truth re-prioritizer.
  10-decisions-log.md:357  [Directive / StatusNote] [Decisions log > 2026-06-20 — Adaptive runtime fusion: the recipe principle, two-tier extensibility, and the Fuel-strategist / backend-synthesizer JIT loop] *Treat a foreign black-box kernel as a new "primitive by declaration."* Rejected — there is no runtime primitive registration and no opaque node; a provider op must decompose into the existing basis or prompt a build-time basis extension.

  10-decisions-log.md:402  [Directive / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-07-03 — The three flagged `decompose`s resolved: NF4 + concrete-`k_len` flash recipes land; symbolic-`k_len` flash + SSM scan are documented basis gaps] **`nf4_matmul` → total primitive recipe.** `dequantize(w_packed, absmax) → matmul`, built with **no data-carrying `Const` and no device handle** (a `decompose` fn has neither): `Cast(U8→F32)` + `lower = wf − 16·⌊wf/16⌋` / `upper = ⌊wf/16⌋` nibble-unpack (exact for `1/16 = 2⁻⁴`), `Unsqueeze→Concat→Reshape` interleave to codes `[N, K]`, a **codebook lookup as an indicator sum** `Σᵢ LUTᵢ·relu(1−|c−i|)` (pure elementwise, exact because codes are exact small integers), broadcast per-block `absmax`, `Transpose`, `MatMul`. Parity test matches the fused CPU byte-kernel numerically.

  10-decisions-log.md:404  [Directive / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-07-03 — The three flagged `decompose`s resolved: NF4 + concrete-`k_len` flash recipes land; symbolic-`k_len` flash + SSM scan are documented basis gaps] **`flash_attn` concrete-`k_len` → total recipe; symbolic-`k_len` → documented gap.** `k_len` is `Option<DynScalar>`. `None` (vanilla) already decomposed. **`Some(Concrete(kl))` was static all along but returned self** (the `k_len.is_some()` short-circuit was too coarse); it now `Slice`s K/V to the live prefix and runs the SDPA recipe **bottom-right-aligned** — `q_pos_offset = kl − Sq` threaded through every causal / sliding-window / ALiBi band (`recompute_probs` + `alibi_bias` gained the offset param; the two existing callers pass 0, keeping the backward byte-identical). **`Some(Sym(_))` is a genuine registry-layer basis gap**: slicing to a *symbolic* length needs a primitive the basis lacks (`Op::Slice` carries a static `usize`; nothing materializes a `DynScalar` into a length-mask tensor inside a `decompose`, which never sees the per-realize `SymEnv`). The symbolic decode *oracle* is emitted one layer up by the optimizer's `decode_flash` arm, which **does** hold the `SymEnv` — so returning self here is correct-by-design, not a punt.

  10-decisions-log.md:406  [Directive / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-07-03 — The three flagged `decompose`s resolved: NF4 + concrete-`k_len` flash recipes land; symbolic-`k_len` flash + SSM scan are documented basis gaps] **`selective_scan` → the constitution's canonical basis gap (G3).** No recipe over today's `Op` basis is both total *and* numerically valid: the `O(seqlen)` unroll is an unbounded, un-re-fusable explosion (not a recipe), and the diagonal-SSM `CumSum` closed-form `h[t] = exp(a·D[t]) ⊙ cumsum_t(exp(−a·D[s]) ⊙ x[s])` **overflows** because Mamba's `a = −exp(a_log) < 0` makes `exp(−a·D[s]) = exp(|a|·D[s])` blow up — exactly why the kernel is chunked. Held as a never-crash surfaced gap; the precise ask to close it is a **build-time `Op`-enum extension** (a higher-order `Scan` / associative- or chunked-scan primitive), per G3.

  10-decisions-log.md:442  [Directive / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-07-04 — FKC reaches all three backends; contracts can pin a cost fn (trampoline); optimize-time kernel-variant selection reads the Judge] **The cost-trampoline (Task-F): an FKC contract can pin a real cost fn.** `CostBlock` gains `cost_fn: Option<String>`; `LinkRegistry` gains `resolve_cost_fn(name) -> Option<CostFn>` (the exact analog of `resolve_primitive`'s symbol→`KernelRef`), so a contract names a registered cost fn and the importer stamps *that* (not the `unknown_cost` sentinel) — `fill_unset_cost_for_backend` then leaves it. This is what let `flash_decoding` migrate while preserving its custom `cost_flash_decoding_cuda` infeasibility gate (returns `flops == u64::MAX` for `seq_q != 1` / `head_dim > 128`), which an unconditional `unknown_cost` → fill_unset upgrade would have destroyed. Unresolvable names are a typed `UnknownCostFn` error, never silent. Demotes the general expression-trampoline (adoption-plan §2.3) to an optional refinement.

  10-decisions-log.md:790  [Named / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-07-15 — `Op::Scan` ships (Phase 1): G3 closed, `decompose` now total over genuine primitives for the whole registered fused-op set] live silent out-of-bounds read
  10-decisions-log.md:828  [Directive / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-07-16 — `Op::Scan` Phase 2: `early_exit` mechanism + BPTT differentiability + Hopfield consumer (two of three obligations closed; slot-1 stays open, no kernel added)] **`early_exit` mechanism — BUILT.** The convergence predicate is carried as one extra **trailing input** when `early_exit = Some` (`inputs = [init_carry, xs.., consts.., body_new_carry, body_y, pred_exit]`); `ScanPredicate` stays a unit marker (its `Some`-ness = "peel one extra trailing input"), so `base_map_hash` / reachability hash the predicate for free and `op_key` needs no new arm. `pred_exit` is a scalar-`U8` sub-DAG over the carry (pre-step via `ScanPlaceholder{Carry,0}`, post-step via the shared `body_new_carry` node). `Tensor::scan_until` is the `Result`-returning builder (build-time predicate shape/dtype/placeholder validation). Forward realize routes a kernel-less early-exit scan to a host **realize-barrier step driver** (`drive_scan_until_final_f32`) that realizes body + predicate per step, feeds the realized carry forward as a fresh `Const`, and stops at the runtime convergence step — a data-dependent count under the static-capacity `bound` (the `03-ir` KV-cache runtime-offset discipline). A non-firing predicate runs to `bound` and stops (bounded, never an infinite loop).
  10-decisions-log.md:828  [Named / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-07-16 — `Op::Scan` Phase 2: `early_exit` mechanism + BPTT differentiability + Hopfield consumer (two of three obligations closed; slot-1 stays open, no kernel added)] `early_exit` mechanism — BUILT.
  10-decisions-log.md:830  [Directive / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-07-16 — `Op::Scan` Phase 2: `early_exit` mechanism + BPTT differentiability + Hopfield consumer (two of three obligations closed; slot-1 stays open, no kernel added)] **BPTT differentiability — BUILT (the generic decompose-backward hook Phase 1 found missing).** `Tensor::backward` runs `lower_scans_for_backward` **before** its reverse topo order: it decomposes the SSM `Op::Fused(SELECTIVE_SCAN|SSD_CHUNK_SCAN)` recipes into `Op::Scan`, `unroll_scan`s every reachable `Op::Scan` to its static `bound` primitives, and rewires the `View` consumers, so the existing node-general reverse walk differentiates only primitives. The `Op::Scan` backward arm becomes a **defensive `panic!` guard** — `backward() -> GradMap` is infallible (no Err channel), so this matches the QMatMul/PagedAttn "no backward" precedent in the same walk; it is unreachable via the public builders (they always emit the `View` consumers the pre-pass's remap + never-hang guard lower away before the walk). The SSM registry `backward` flips `NotDifferentiable → Decompose` — but per the Phase-1 entry's own "**`BackwardKind` is verified never read**" finding, this flip is **intent documentation, not the mechanism** (the pre-pass replaces the nodes before the walk). BPTT is **truncated to the static `bound`**: the runtime `early_exit` predicate is forward-only and ignored by the build-time backward unroll (no implicit-function-theorem / deep-equilibrium gradients). Two real gaps surfaced and were fixed on the primitive path: the `Op::Concat` backward arm was binary-only (`unroll_scan`'s stacked-`ys` is N-ary — 1-input for seqlen 1) → generalized to N-ary; and a decomposed multi-output fused op's surviving slot-0 `View` emitted an `Op::ScatterIntoSlot` (no CPU realize kernel) into the gradient graph → the pre-pass now collapses slot-0 `View` consumers directly to the decompose output (slot 0 == the primary value per the multi-output authoring contract).
  10-decisions-log.md:830  [Named / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-07-16 — `Op::Scan` Phase 2: `early_exit` mechanism + BPTT differentiability + Hopfield consumer (two of three obligations closed; slot-1 stays open, no kernel added)] `BackwardKind` is verified never read
  10-decisions-log.md:832  [Directive / StatusNote] [Decisions log > 2026-07-16 — `Op::Scan` Phase 2: `early_exit` mechanism + BPTT differentiability + Hopfield consumer (two of three obligations closed; slot-1 stays open, no kernel added)] **slot-1 (`last_state`) view — STILL OPEN, deliberately.** Phase 2 added **no `Op::Scan` native kernel** — Hopfield forward runs via the step-by-step unroll driver, BPTT via the unroll pre-pass, both over primitives that already have kernels. So the 2026-07-15 entry's build-order constraint is honored by *not triggering it*: with no kernel, the slot-1/`last_state` OOB stays a typed dispatch error, not a live silent out-of-bounds read. Wiring or dropping the slot-1 view (an `Op::ScatterIntoSlot` composer) remains the prerequisite for any *future* `Op::Scan` kernel — this phase neither needs nor resolves it.

One shape authority per primitive → no builder-vs-emit drift (a `builders_agree_with_primitive_shape` no-drift guard locks it). It returns an honest `Err` (never panics) for basis-gap / higher-order / malformed inputs.

  10-decisions-log.md:940  [Named / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-07-28 — The consumer contract (new section 15) + withdrawal of the "inference center of gravity" claim] `09-non-goals` v0.3 → v0.4 (MAJOR core-claim correction)
  10-decisions-log.md:940  [Named / StatusNote] [Decisions log > 2026-07-28 — The consumer contract (new section 15) + withdrawal of the "inference center of gravity" claim] withdrawn as wrong
  10-decisions-log.md:947  [Directive / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-07-28 — The consumer contract (new section 15) + withdrawal of the "inference center of gravity" claim] **The rule and its derivation.** Fuel owns mechanism; the consumer owns policy. The discriminator is three-way: selection among *equivalent implementations* is fuel's (arm selection), selection among *competing work* is the consumer's (value, not cost), selection among *different math* is the consumer's. The line is *derived* from cost fungibility rather than asserted — a layer can only optimize over a fungible cost unit, and fuel cannot compare one caller's work against another's.
  10-decisions-log.md:948  [Directive / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-07-28 — The consumer contract (new section 15) + withdrawal of the "inference center of gravity" claim] **Seven clauses**: C-1 capacity advertisement, C-2 bounded quantum + cancellation, C-3 state externalization (with a lossy-vs-exact fidelity axis), C-4 measured cost and provenance, C-5 constraint admission (determinism/tolerance/device set prune the arm set), C-6 observation and intervention, C-7 declared mutation and in-process invalidation. Plus the matching refusals (no fairness/SLA, no admission, no work lifecycle, no multi-tenancy policy, no batching or eviction policy).
  10-decisions-log.md:949  [Directive / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-07-28 — The consumer contract (new section 15) + withdrawal of the "inference center of gravity" claim] **Scope pin**: the contract governs *execution* consumers only. Export, surgery, merging, pruning, visualization, and static analysis consume the IR, not the runtime; they are governed by [03-ir](03-ir.md) + [13-interchange](13-interchange.md), and this contract is not to be grown to cover them.

  10-decisions-log.md:1475  [Named / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-08-12 — A decline carries TWO axes: what KIND of token it is, and WHY the capability is absent. They compose as two fields and never merge.] `StorageStatus` literally cannot be called on `"f8e4m3fnuz"`.
  10-decisions-log.md:1483  [Directive / StatusNote] [Decisions log > 2026-08-12 — A decline carries TWO axes: what KIND of token it is, and WHY the capability is absent. They compose as two fields and never merge.] **A `decline_pending!` macro** — REJECTED. A macro can record a reason but cannot observe the capability arriving, so it produces better *documentation* of a hazard. Documentation is not a control; this is the same finding as the runtime-skip program (GAP-157), where a skip that declared itself in prose was invisible.
  10-decisions-log.md:1484  [Directive / StatusNote] [Decisions log > 2026-08-12 — A decline carries TWO axes: what KIND of token it is, and WHY the capability is absent. They compose as two fields and never merge.] **A canary over a hand-declared `(backend, dtype)` pair list** — REJECTED, and it was the architect's own proposal. It puts the decline (the arm) and its declaration (a list in a test) in **two separate acts**, which is exactly the state KISS #141 abolished with *"the skip mechanism and the declaration are now the same act."* Worse, its predicate could not observe the event: several dtypes already exist as raw-byte `CudaSlice<u8>` variants, so a canary asserting "no typed variant" would never move if storage arrived by that route. And the key was ill-typed — the same dtype is *present* at `transfer_to_device` and *declining* at a compute op, so storage and kernel are two populations.
  10-decisions-log.md:1485  [Directive / StatusNote] [Decisions log > 2026-08-12 — A decline carries TWO axes: what KIND of token it is, and WHY the capability is absent. They compose as two fields and never merge.] **A single flat enum** — REJECTED per the domain argument above.
  10-decisions-log.md:1495  [Directive / Description] [Decisions log > 2026-08-12 — A decline carries TWO axes: what KIND of token it is, and WHY the capability is absent. They compose as two fields and never merge.] **The storage authority is strong, not airtight.** Without a macro co-generating the `CudaStorageSlice` variants and the `Present` arms from one list, someone can wire storage via a non-gated site without flipping the central classification. The macro form is a later increment; the limit sits in `cuda_storage_status`'s own doc comment.
  10-decisions-log.md:1497  [Directive / StatusNote] [Decisions log > 2026-08-12 — A decline carries TWO axes: what KIND of token it is, and WHY the capability is absent. They compose as two fields and never merge.] **Sabotage coverage is recorded at the level it reaches.** The mechanism is sabotage-validated; the four repointed sites' live runtime paths are not — they are device-gated, and are covered instead by compile-level routing (the sites demonstrably call the shared helper; the old hardcoded errors are gone) plus transitive classification corruption. Closing that with a live GPU test was **declined**: it would rest on a fragile `Display`-through-`fuel_ir::Error` assertion, and a weak test where a compile-level proof already exists is worse than none.
